Codiluce compatibility · P10d2b2b2b2c6b2 · 12 October 2026

Original production cache and performance

All six original production projects retain complete logical graph parity across cold indexing, cache population and warm replay. Median cold and warm indexing time and sampled process-tree memory pass the unchanged 10% comparison gates.

LOCAL HEAD CACHE/PERFORMANCE SLICE QUALIFIED
All 195 original revisions, real symlink profiles, wider source accuracy, semantic/compiler/WASM budgets and native Windows/macOS execution remain open. The full compatibility goal stays active.

Unmodified performance gates

Every project passes both cold and warm time/RSS guardrails, with complete cross-producer, cross-mode and cross-repetition graph parity and actual Git-metrics cache proof. The previous failed 108-process observation is preserved separately. This replacement changes the cause: validated immutable Rust evidence snapshots, admitted table-index reuse, original proof memoization and exact original shallow Git boundaries.

Predecessor a827759 and current runtime bd86e60 use identical inputs, owned dependencies, cache defaults and configurations. Three repetitions alternate producer order per project. Each cold, population and warm run starts a fresh process; only the named population/warm cache is shared. Medians must remain at or below 1.10× for both indexing time and sampled process-tree RSS. Population results are retained and required for parity.

RSS is sampled externally every 25 ms across all-thread descendants during indexing. Shared pages may be counted repeatedly, and polling or exiting processes can miss peaks. Parent VmHWM, sampled child peaks, process counts and incomplete samples are retained. The sampler stops and acknowledges its final boundary before logical digest/source verification begins. This is a relative guardrail, not an absolute memory ceiling.

Complete proofs and actual cache behavior

Every original regular file is rehashed against its materialization receipt and Git blob identity before and after each run. Whole logical JSON is hashed in bounded 64 KiB chunks, including every entity, relation, diagnostic, unknown field, condition, source coordinate, repeated proof occurrence and array position. Reviewed equivalence removes only run UUID/time, normalizes Rust symbol producer 2 to 1 and reconstructs exact ordered proofs from file-local tables. Every project must agree across both producers, all modes and all repetitions; independent strict source anchors must also pass.

The working-tree API exercises real caches and includes original Git metrics. Revision mode intentionally disables caching. Isolated owned HEAD/config/index metadata preserves exact original shallow boundaries and reads original Git objects at the reviewed pins; target Git config, hooks, filters, dependencies and code never execute. Nine original symlinks remain explicitly skipped in the regular-file profile. Missing symlinks can make the isolated working-tree status dirty; that field remains compared. The working-tree graphs include Git metrics, so they are a separate profile from the previous revision-only saved graphs.

Default limits remain two entries per unit, 256 MiB total compressed cache and 64 MiB decoded JSON per entry. A unit exceeding those limits falls back to indexing; warm replay does not imply every analyzer is cached. Actual hit/miss events remain visible above and in the raw receipt. Successful cache bytes are removed only after complete cross-producer/cross-mode parity; their compressed-byte SHA-256/size/name ledgers are retained locally. Failed or unequal units retain their actual cache.

Corrected shallow metadata and preserved failures

The metadata preflight retains BookStack’s 12 and Firefly III’s eight original shallow boundaries. All six bounded original Git logs are readable before timing. Every warm run must record a real Git-metrics cache hit; language/framework hits and misses remain recorded separately. No previous observation is overwritten. The failed comparison retains Axum’s earlier 48.02% cold-time / 13.74% cold-RSS / 30.97% warm-time regressions and the previous missing-Git-metrics classifications.

Current source/package qualification is the immutable evidence implementation phase: 1,748 passing tests, engine/web typechecks, 6.89 MB/369-file installed CLI/API/history/fallback/signal/npm-exec checks, and exact complete saved graph-body equality for all six originals. Development Git-init and symbol-getter failures remain linked there. This phase adds the actual repeated original-head cache/performance result.

Preserved source gaps

Graph parity preserves the current static analysis scope and its diagnostics. It does not establish complete production accuracy. For example, three original Firefly III files retain PHP parse errors, including a pipe-operator version rejection. Both PHP cache units decline to store unqualified source results; warm runs reanalyze them. These source failures are distinct from worker failures and remain part of every compared graph.
